#### A4. Distribution, baseline, score arithmetic and sensitivity

**Previous centre calculation, transcribed:**

1. Average pace: `(99.3 + 99.9)/2 = 99.6` possessions.
2. OKC vs SAS efficiency centre: `99.6 × (118.9 + 111.3) / 200 = 114.64` points.
3. SAS vs OKC efficiency centre: `99.6 × (119.6 + 107.7) / 200 = 113.20` points.
4. Unadjusted combined centre `227.84`. San Antonio home margin edge `+1.71`, allocated `+0.855` SAS / `−0.855` OKC, yields OKC `113.78`, SAS `114.05`, margin approximately SAS `+0.27`.
5. NBA opening-game multiplier in the prior repo baseline: `0.988 × 227.84 ≈ 225.10`. Prior L20 combined averages were OKC `226.8` vs regular season `226.9` (−0.1), Spurs `217.6` vs regular season `231.3` (−13.7). Subjective 20% average-difference shrinkage: `0.20 × [(−0.1 −13.7)/2] ≈ −1.38`. Base total `225.10 − 1.38 ≈ 223.72`.
6. Previous analysis's three scenarios (low/base/high) weighted `0.25/0.50/0.25`:

| Scenario | Weight | Total mean μT | Spurs signed margin mean μM | Width σT | Width σM |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Low scoring / OKC edge | 0.25 | 218.00 | −3.00 | 21.7 | 16.4 |
| Base | 0.50 | 223.72 | +0.27 | 21.7 | 16.4 |
| Higher scoring / SAS edge | 0.25 | 231.00 | +3.50 | 21.7 | 16.4 |

Widths 21.7 and 16.4 came from **raw** prior NBA 2025–26 SDs rather than narrower residual widths 19.4/15.1. No opponent-fitted conditional variance was claimed. The score arithmetic alone cannot produce an empirically verified total–margin covariance; a precise joint distribution is unavailable.

**Original normal-CDF calculations:**

- `P(Under 229.5) = Σ w_i Φ((229.5−μTi)/21.7)` = `0.25×0.702 + 0.50×0.605 + 0.25×0.472 ≈ 0.596`.
- `P(Spurs +1.5) = Σ w_i Φ((1.5+μMi)/16.4)` = `0.25×0.464 + 0.50×0.543 + 0.25×0.620 ≈ 0.542`.
- `P(Over 229.5) ≈ 0.404`; `P(Thunder −1.5) ≈ 0.458`, exact complements conditional on the assumed rules.
- `P(Spurs win) = 0.25×0.427 + 0.50×0.506 + 0.25×0.585 ≈ 0.506` with an approximate normal-no-tie final-winner calculation. `P(Thunder win) ≈ 0.494`.
- Base-only probabilities: Under ≈0.605, Spurs +1.5 ≈0.543, Thunder −1.5 ≈0.457, Over ≈0.395.
- Weighted mixture score centre: `0.25×218 + 0.50×223.72 + 0.25×231 = 224.11`; weighted Spurs margin `0.25×(−3) + 0.50×0.27 + 0.25×3.5 = 0.26`. Implied SAS `(224.11+0.26)/2 ≈112.19`, OKC `(224.11−0.26)/2 ≈111.93`. This is a derived expectation, **not** a new forecast or an empirical fitted model.

**Same-line league-only comparator (unverified frozen status):** NBA 2025–26 mean total 230.7; early multiplier 0.988 gives `230.7 ×0.988 = 227.9316`, raw total SD 21.7 → Under 229.5 ≈ `Φ((229.5−227.9316)/21.7)` = 0.529. League home edge +1.71; raw margin SD 16.4 → Spurs +1.5 ≈ `Φ((1.5+1.71)/16.4)` = 0.578. Complements are 0.471 and 0.422. These are **diagnostic recreation**, not proof of original baseline freezing.

**Sensitivity from earlier answer:** Low total centre 218 → Under 70.2%; base 223.72 → 60.5%; high 231 → 47.2%; weighted 59.6%. Interpretation: lower-scoring defensive/pacing regimes support Under, but an offensive reset reverses that preference. These are scenarios, not confidence intervals.

**Endpoint simplification:** Historical total distribution contains observed NBA OT games; no fresh +25-point OT correction was added. The exact regulation-tie mass and OT transition were **not** enumerated, and probability numbers must not be presented as an exact discrete/OT model.

League anchor (2026/27, 80 matches 22 Aug – 21 Oct 12:30Z, both teams in the league, target excluded): 216 goals = 2.700 per match; home 1.750, away 0.950 per match; LM = 1.350 goals per team-game; home edge HE = +0.800 (margin SD 2.015, SE 0.225); total SD 1.626; first-half share s = 89 first-half goals / 213 goals in the 79 matches with a half-time score = **0.4178**; results home 50, draw 16, away 14.

TB-1-MD (PROBABILITY_TOOLKIT §4.2, k = 2):
- Potkozarje: O_h = (12 + 2×1.35)/13 = 1.1308; D_h = (12 + 2.7)/13 = 1.1308.
- Drina: O_a = (23 + 2.7)/13 = 1.9769; D_a = (9 + 2.7)/13 = 0.9000.
- T = (1.1308 + 0.9000)/2 + (1.9769 + 1.1308)/2 = 2.5692.
- M = [(1.1308 − 1.1308) − (1.9769 − 0.9000)]/2 + 0.800 = +0.2615.
- λ_home = (T + M)/2 = **1.4154**; λ_away = (T − M)/2 = **1.1538**; first half 0.4178 × each = 0.591 / 0.482 (total 1.073).

Mixture: low ×0.85 (1.203 / 0.981), base ×1.00, high ×1.15 (1.628 / 1.327), weights 0.25/0.50/0.25. There is no diversification and Hit@2 is effectively one bet. Rank 1 and Rank 4 (different quantities, total and margin, independent within each case): both lose 0.1112.

Without the EARLY_SEASON 0.988 multiplier the total centre is 231.67 and the order becomes Under 239.5 (0.6540), Hawks +6.5 (0.6239), Under 237.5 (0.6160), Under 235.5 (0.5768). #### A4. Distribution, baseline and arithmetic
**League level.** ESPN standings, 2025-26 regular season, 30 teams: league points per team-game LM = 115.607 (game total 231.21). Earlier seasons for the regression: 2024-25 113.83 (227.65); 2023-24 114.21 (228.42). Repo reference (BASE_RATES_REGISTER §7.1, NBA 2025-26): mean total 230.7, home margin +1.72 (95% CI 0.81–2.64), raw SD total 21.7, margin 16.4, residual SD 19.4 / 15.1, overtime in 4.4% of games adding 25.2 points on average. The 0.5-point gap between 231.21 and 230.7 is disclosed and not reconciled.

**Carry-over (measured, ESPN standings).** On league-centred points for and against per game, regressing the next season on the previous one, 30 teams per pair: offence 0.362 (2023-24 → 2024-25) and 0.266 (2024-25 → 2025-26), mean **0.314**; defence 0.543 and 0.736, mean **0.640**. Residual team SD of (PF+PA) 6.08 and 4.77 (mean 5.43); of (PF−PA) 4.94 and 5.39 (mean 5.17).

**Team centres.** Deviations from LM in 2025-26: Atlanta PF +2.85, PA +0.44; Orlando PF +0.13, PA −0.50. Carried: Atlanta o′ = 0.314 × 2.85 = +0.897, d′ = 0.640 × 0.44 = +0.283; Orlando o′ = 0.314 × 0.13 = +0.043, d′ = 0.640 × (−0.50) = −0.318.

**Centre (TB-1-MD form, Orlando home).** T = 2 × 115.607 + (0.043 + 0.283 + 0.897 − 0.318)/2 = 231.214 + 0.453 = **231.667**; × EARLY_SEASON 0.988 = **228.886**. M = [(0.043 + 0.318) − (0.897 − 0.283)]/2 + 1.72 = −0.127 + 1.72 = **+1.593** (Orlando). Expected score Orlando 115.24, Atlanta 113.65.

**Mixture and widths.** Offsets from the regression residuals: total d = √2 × (√2 × 5.43/2) = ±5.43; margin d = √2 × (√2 × 5.17/2) = ±5.17. Nine states (total case × margin case), weights 0.25/0.50/0.25 each, independent. Within each state the total and the margin are independent normals with SD 19.4 and 15.1 (correlation between total and margin assumed 0; not measured here). Effective SD total √(19.4² + 0.5 × 5.43²) = **19.78**; margin √(15.1² + 0.5 × 5.17²) = **15.54**. Check against the width rule: 19.78 ≥ 0.85 × 19.4 = 16.5. Row probabilities: Under L = Σ w Φ((L − μT)/19.4); Magic −k = Σ w [1 − Φ((k − μM)/15.1)]; Hawks +k is its complement; Magic win k = 0.

**Baseline (BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1, league-only, frozen).** Total ~ N(230.7 × 0.988 = 227.93, 21.7); margin ~ N(+1.72, 16.4). Example: Under 233.5 = Φ((233.5 − 227.93)/21.7) = Φ(0.257) = 0.6013; Hawks +2.5 = Φ((2.5 − 1.72)/16.4) = Φ(0.048) = 0.5190. `TEAM_BASELINE_P`: `TB1_EARLY_SEASON` (both teams have 0 games in this season), so TB-1-MD reduces to the league-only baseline and is not separately printed.

**Departure ledger (§8).** No priced row departs from BASELINE_P by more than 0.020. #### A7. Family rules and dependence
Rank 1 is a totals Under: width rule K-13 satisfied (effective SD 19.78 ≥ 19.4). Nested Unders are one view, so the primary slate is two views at most (a total view and the Hawks +6.5 margin view); the framework counts only Ranks 1–2 as primary. Underdog cushions (C-PLUS-CUSHION): Hawks +k = P(Hawks win) + P(lose by ≤ k) (0.4592 + 0.0641 = 0.5233 for +2.5). The repo's weaker-team cover bands are 0.35–0.43 for +2.5, 0.38–0.45 for +3.5 and 0.44–0.51 for +5.5. The Hawks rows exceed those bands by more than 0.05 (+2.5: 0.5233; +5.5: 0.5992; +6.5: 0.6239) and no favourite-side mechanism supports a cushion, so Rank 4 carries the flag `PLUS_CUSHION_UNSUPPORTED` as disclosure. The bands describe weaker teams in typical matchups; this is a near pick'em (the league-only BASELINE_P for the same rows, 0.5190 / 0.5911 / 0.6147, also exceeds them), so the excess comes from the matchup, not from a cushion argument. The RM-1 pair flip is non-operative since v10 (ranking is by p_card) and is not applied. BK-P1 (rules): NBA 48 minutes, five-minute overtime. BK-P2 (availability): official injury lists yes; official starting fives and bench no → rows depending on a named player are not issued. BK-P3/P4: all rows full game including overtime, operator terms unknown. No ML or RL output was read before this card (MLSCOPE-v2).

#### A7. Rules, family and mechanical checks

NBA four12-minute quarters,5-minute OT until winner; sixth personal foul ordinarily disqualifies. Team penalty after4 regulation-quarter team fouls /3 OT, with last-two-minute exceptions. Current official rules A8. No referee/foul coefficient. Official assignment page at23:05Z lists John Goble,Natalie Sago,CJ Washington; pre-start publication not proved, verification only, no input. Indoor/no weather effect.

All ranked rows full-game team quantities; no supported player/period rows. Same-grid winner/handicap/total complements and monotonicity verified. WAS+14.5=P(WASwin)+P(lose by1..14), not a league absolute-margin statistic. Rank1 Under SD24.111 exceeds fresh raw19.876 and historical reference19.4. Risks: BASKETBALL_TOTAL_WITHOUT_PACE_MODEL / UNFITTED_ANALYST_ADJUSTMENT / SMALL_SAMPLE_STRENGTH_OVERREACH; limitations, not retrospective causal findings.

## Card · LOCAL-20261022F6K3OJNO-G0005 · ICE HOCKEY / AHL 2026-27 regular season · Hamilton Hammers @ Toronto Marlies · 21 Oct 2026 (Toronto) / 22 Oct 2026 (AEDT)

- **Local event key:** `LOCAL-20261022F6K3OJNO-G0005` — `LOCAL_UNIMPORTED_EVENT`.

- **Probability method:** League anchor from the full 2025-26 AHL season (all 1,152 regular-season games, final scores); team centres from TB-1-MD arithmetic on 2026-27 goals for and against (6 games each) shrunk toward a measured season-to-season carry-over of 2025-26 (k = 20, the NHL/MLB constant, `UNVALIDATED` for the AHL); the league distribution of final totals tilted to the matchup mean; a normal on the final margin anchored on the empirical home-win rate; empirical puck-line shares; nine-state mixture (0.25/0.50/0.25 on the total centre and on the margin centre, widths from the carry-over residuals and the measured goalie-talent spread). Computed with code; every input and step is in A4.
- **Scheduled start:** **2026-10-21 19:00 EDT = 23:00:00Z = 2026-10-22 10:00 AEDT** (HockeyTech schedule, `GameDateISO8601` 2026-10-21T19:00:00-04:00; matches the owner's estimate). Venue: the home arena is not shown by the feeds I read (`NOT_RETRIEVED`); the Marlies are the home team.
- **Timing:** Request received 2026-10-22T10:04:53+11:00 (23:04:53Z), **4 minutes 53 seconds after the scheduled start**. The only target-specific facts read were the schedule row (23:05:31Z: kickoff time and status "7:00 pm EDT, started 0, final 0", read as feed lag, not as a delay) and the game-centre preview (23:06:22Z: team standings lines, previous meetings, no line-ups). Everything else is dated 2026-10-20 or earlier. No score, goal, shot count, line-up or live state of this game was read; target rows were removed by game id before any field was parsed. Roster and transaction snapshots were read at 23:12–23:14Z (newest entry 2026-10-20). Label: `CLEAN_PRE_START_INPUTS / POST_START_ISSUE` (own research cohort; not a pre-start-issued forecast).
- **Endpoint / definition:** AHL regular season: 60 minutes, a five-minute overtime (goalie minutes 65:00 in the shootout game 1029105), then a shootout. The official final score gives the shootout winner one extra goal. **Moneylines:** two readings frozen and queued as an operator question: (R-A) full game, overtime and shootout included, no draw; (R-B) 60-minute regulation, a tie loses. **Totals at 5.5:** identical under every reading (a tie after 60 minutes has an even total, at most 4 or at least 6, and one overtime goal or the shootout credit never crosses 5.5). Puck lines are derived on the official final score.
- **Lineup state:** Starting goalies and line-ups `LINEUP_UNCONFIRMED` for both sides (AHL game sheets exist only once the game is under way; the preview line-up field is empty). The AHL publishes no injury report. Both teams have new head coaches; the Marlies kept 9 of the 20 players they dressed in their 2025-26 finale, and the Hammers' roster continuity is unknown.
- **Learning record:** 12 dated 2026-27 team rows with goalies (A3), roster status counts, transactions with dates, and the league population (A4). Availability for skaters is unknown, not healthy.

### Decision block

Two high-event, near-average teams. The model puts the Marlies a slight favourite (home edge plus a better start: 4-2 against 1-4-1) and the total about a quarter-goal above the league. Departures from the league-only baseline are small (at most 0.046). The strongest rows are cushions and a far Under, not a view on either side.

| Rank | Role | Tag | Contract key / exact proposition | p_card | p_base | BASELINE_P | Line distance (units / z) | Probability basis | Main failure route |
|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | PICK (RANK1_UNSTABLE) | ANALYST_DERIVED | AHL:FULL:MARLIES:+1.5:FINAL_SCORE | 0.7418 | 0.7422 | 0.7248 | margin line −1.5 − centre +0.34 = −1.84 goals; z −0.70 | Mixture, margin | Hammers win by two or more; 73% of two-goal NHL regulation wins contain an empty-net goal (AHL share not measured), so a late empty-netter is the kill path |
| 2 | PICK | ANALYST_DERIVED | AHL:FULL:TOTAL:UNDER:7.5:FINAL_SCORE | 0.7237 | 0.7249 | 0.7639 | 7.5 − 6.40 = +1.10 goals; z +0.47 | Mixture, total | A game of eight or more goals (both teams have averaged 7.2 in 2026-27) |
| 3 | INFORMATIONAL | ANALYST_DERIVED | AHL:FULL:HAMMERS:+1.5:FINAL_SCORE | 0.6694 | 0.6691 | 0.6875 | margin line +1.5 − centre +0.34 = +1.16 goals; z +0.44 | Mixture, margin | Marlies win by two or more |
| 4 | INFORMATIONAL | SUPPLIED | AHL:FULL:TOTAL:OVER:5.5:FINAL_SCORE | 0.5934 | 0.5952 | 0.5495 | 5.5 − 6.40 = −0.90 goals; z −0.39 | Mixture, total | Five goals or fewer (a 2-2 tie after 60 minutes lands here) |

**Baseline coverage:** 17/17 priced rows and 4/4 ranked rows (BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1: literal 2025-26 AHL league frequencies on the identical line, n = 1,152, frozen in this card before pricing).
**Rank-1 gate:** `RANK1_UNSTABLE`. p 0.7418 ≥ 0.62; the best non-complementary alternative in the eligible priced universe is Under 7.5 at 0.7237: gap 0.0181 < 0.04. The grid is exact to about 1e-6, so this is not a precision tie. **Would-be Rank 1 removed:** Over 4.5 (0.8092) was the top row, but it is a total with both starting goalies unresolved in an early-season competition (IH-P2, control 14); it is removed from the primary selection and kept in the diagnostic ladder, as SELECTION_RULES §2 requires.
**`SHADOW_GATE_RET03`:** excluding every signed-margin row (winner and handicap rows share that quantity), the alternative is Under 7.5 at 0.7237: gap 0.0181 → RANK1_UNSTABLE (shadow only).
**Rank-2 floor:** PASS (0.7237 ≥ 0.58).
**Joint failure:** Ranks 1 and 2 are on different quantities; exact joint loss 0.0713 (the total and the margin are assumed independent within each centre state; their correlation was not measured). Ranks 1 and 3 are opposite +1.5 lines: they cannot both lose (`MECHANICALLY_PROTECTED`), a covering pair whose combined record carries no skill; only Ranks 1–2 count as primary.
**Shadow gate / Rank 2:** RET04 (highest p ≥ 0.58 on a different quantity from Rank 1) = Under 7.5, joint with Rank 1 0.0713. XCARD6 (minimum exact joint failure with Rank 1 among p ≥ 0.58) = Hammers +1.5, 0.6694, joint 0.0000 (disjoint, `MECHANICALLY_PROTECTED`, same quantity). NOT_PRIMARY.
**Shadow RANK_DELTA / BASE_ONLY:** RANK_DELTA top two (p ≥ 0.58, by p_card − BASELINE_P, eligible rows) = Over 5.5 (+0.0439) and Marlies +1.5 (+0.0170), joint loss 0.1050. BASE_ONLY (base case only): same order and gate (0.7422 / 0.7249 / 0.6691 / 0.5952; gap 0.0173, RANK1_UNSTABLE). NOT_PRIMARY.
**Adjustment dependence:** `ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT`. On the league-only baseline the first two ranks would be Under 7.5 (0.7623) then Marlies +1.5 (0.7248): the team model swaps them. The same swap occurs with k = 40 and with the 2025-26 prior alone (A6). The set of the top three rows is stable; their order is not.
**Potential winner:** **Toronto Marlies 0.5464; Hamilton Hammers 0.4536** (two-way, overtime and shootout included, R-A). On the regulation reading (R-B): Marlies 0.4300, tie after 60 minutes 0.2326, Hammers 0.3373. A lean to the home side, not a confident call.

**The four supplied rows (all assessed):**

| Supplied row | R-A p_card | BASELINE_P | R-B p_card (draw loses) | Position |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| **Hammers ML** | 0.4536 | 0.4835 | 0.3373 | 9th of 17 |
| **Marlies ML** | 0.5464 | 0.5165 | 0.4300 | 6th of 17 |

Only Over 5.5 is above 0.58; the model's Over lean comes from two sources, the high 2026-27 scoring of both teams (7.2 goals per game over 6 games, 3 of them against each other) and a league mean that was already 6.13 with 55% of games reaching six goals. The Marlies moneyline is 0.03 above the baseline on the home edge and the better start.
**Supplied rows not selected:** Marlies ML, Hammers ML and Under 5.5, for the reasons above; none was removed to manufacture a pass.
**Player rows:** none. No line-ups are published and no skater-level minutes, usage or injury data were retrieved; a player prop would be invented.

**Model shadow (MLSCOPE-v2, frozen after this card):** run only after this card was written and read back; the dated shadow record is appended at the end of this card.

#### A4. Distribution, baseline and arithmetic
**League anchor (AHL 2025-26 regular season, all 1,152 games from 2025-10-07 to 2026-04-19, HockeyTech schedule, `final` = 1, final scores):** mean total 6.1328 (LM = 3.0664 per team-game), total SD 2.305, home 3.138 and away 2.995 per game (home edge HE = +0.1432; standard error about 0.077), home-win rate 0.5165, margin SD 2.608, games reaching overtime 0.2326 (175 decided in overtime, 93 in a shootout), regulation home wins 0.4019 and away wins 0.3655, share of home wins by 2+ 0.6050 and of away wins by 2+ 0.5691, total = 5 in 0.2309 and = 6 in 0.0972 of games. 2023-24 and 2024-25 league means were 6.061 and 6.060.

**Carry-over (measured, 32 teams per pair, HockeyTech schedules for seasons 81, 86, 90):** goals-for slope 0.425 (2023-24 → 2024-25) and 0.323 (2024-25 → 2025-26), mean **0.374**; goals-against slope 0.420 and 0.458, mean **0.439**. Residual team SD of (GF + GA): 0.320 and 0.302 (mean 0.311); of (GF − GA): 0.438 and 0.544 (mean 0.491).

**Ratings (k = 20):** 2025-26 deviations from LM: Marlies GF +0.114, GA +0.100; Bridgeport/Hammers GF −0.025, GA +0.017. Prior = slope × deviation: Marlies +0.0426 / +0.0439; Hammers −0.0094 / +0.0075. Current 2026-27 deviations (6 games): Marlies GF 23/6 − 3.0664 = +0.7669, GA 20/6 − 3.0664 = +0.2669; Hammers GF 18/6 − 3.0664 = −0.0664, GA 25/6 − 3.0664 = +1.1003. Weights 6/26 current, 20/26 prior. Marlies: o = 0.2308 × 0.7669 + 0.7692 × 0.0426 = **+0.2098**, d = 0.2308 × 0.2669 + 0.7692 × 0.0439 = **+0.0954**. Hammers: o = **−0.0225**, d = 0.2308 × 1.1003 + 0.7692 × 0.0075 = **+0.2596**.

**Centre (Toronto home):** T = 2 × 3.0664 + (0.2098 + 0.2596 − 0.0225 + 0.0954)/2 = 6.1328 + 0.2712 = **6.4040** goals; M = [(0.2098 − 0.0954) − (−0.0225 − 0.2596)]/2 + 0.1432 = 0.1983 + 0.1432 = **+0.3415** (Marlies). Expected final score Marlies 3.37, Hammers 3.03.

**Mixture and widths.** Centre standard deviations: total √[(√2 × 0.311/2)² + (√2 × 0.172)²] = 0.328, margin √[(√2 × 0.491/2)² + (√2 × 0.172)²] = 0.424 (carry-over residual plus goalie talent). Three-point offsets with weights 0.25/0.50/0.25: total ±0.464 goals, margin ±0.600 goals, nine states, independent. Effective SD total 2.328, margin 2.642 (league 2.305 / 2.608).

**Row formulas.** Totals: the league pmf of final totals (n = 1,152) tilted by e^(θt) so its mean equals the state's total centre; Over 5.5 = P(total ≥ 6) (0.5952 in the base state). Winner: p_home = Φ[Φ⁻¹(0.5165) + (M − 0.1432)/2.608] (0.5470 in the base state); away = 1 − home. Puck lines: P(Marlies −1.5) = p_home × 0.6050; P(Hammers −1.5) = (1 − p_home) × 0.5691; the +1.5 rows are the complements. Regulation: Marlies = p_home − 0.2326 × 0.5; tie = 0.2326 (league rate, not adjusted); Hammers = 1 − p_home − 0.2326 × 0.5. p_card = Σ states weight × row.

**Baseline (BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1, frozen):** literal 2025-26 AHL frequencies on the identical line, e.g. total ≥ 6 in 633 of 1,152 games = 0.5495; home win 0.5165; Marlies +1.5 = 1 − (1 − 0.5165) × 0.5691 = 0.7248. `TEAM_BASELINE_P`: TB-1-MD for the AHL has no validated constants (nearest sport: NHL, k = 20); the NHL rule that TB-1 has no resolution and the row anchors on BASELINE_P is followed, so the card stays within 0.046 of BASELINE_P everywhere.

**Departure ledger (§8).** The team model differs from the baseline in three places: the total centre is +0.27 goals (both teams scoring more in 2026-27), the margin centre is +0.20 over the home edge, and the effective SDs are 1% wider. Resulting departures by row: Over 5.5 +0.0439, Marlies ML +0.0299, Under 7.5 −0.0402, Marlies +1.5 +0.0170.

**Checks (toolkit §11).** Complements sum to 1: Over + Under 5.5 = 0.5934 + 0.4066; Marlies ML + Hammers ML = 0.5464 + 0.4536; Marlies regulation + tie + Hammers regulation = 0.4300 + 0.2326 + 0.3373 = 0.9999 (rounding); the +1.5 rows and the Over/Under rows come from the same objects. The standings lines in the preview feed (goals exclude the shootout credit: Marlies 22–20, Hammers 18–24) agree with the schedule rows (23–20 and 18–25) once the credit in the 10-09 shootout game is removed.

#### A4. Numerical analyst procedure and reproducibility

**Not ML/RL.** `python -m ml capability` exited nonzero (`No module named ml`), so exact-competition CYCLE-v1 release, fitted primary grid, source-known-at Hedge feedback, frozen baseline and execution hashes are **not** run/captured. No false expert loss, calibration, probability certificate or model receipt.

**Analyst assumptions:** full 60m score distribution `G_BEI ~ Poisson(λB)` and `G_KUL ~ Poisson(λK)` independently with λB=2.272222, λK=3.355000, then regulation draws resolved using a one-awarded-goal endpoint. OT winner allocation proportional to λK/(λB+λK)=0.596209; this allocation is **arbitrary modelling assumption**, not an observed SO skill. Empty-net and goalie-pull correlations not modelled; season-rate uncertainty larger than Poisson width. The supplied lines are evaluated on the same grid.

**One transparent centre, no claim of fitted optimal weights:** 70% season-versus-opponent averages and 30% analogous L10 comparisons; windows overlap and never serve as two independent samples. For Beibarys, season centre `(38/18 + 48/18)/2 = 2.388889`, L10 centre `(17/10 + 23/10)/2 = 2.000000`, composite `0.7×2.388889 +0.3×2.000000 = 2.272222`. For Kulager season `(60/18 +57/18)/2 = 3.250000`, L10 `(33/10+39/10)/2 = 3.600000`, composite `0.7×3.250000+0.3×3.600000=3.355000`. These weights are analyst assumptions, not estimated or confirmed league/profile coefficients. Baseline league-only p `NOT_COMPUTED`, because exact PHL historical league distribution/frozen point-in-time profile not obtained. No cross-league NHL substitution.

**Regulation goals / full-match tie branch:** sum reg total is Poisson(5.627222); regulation 3–3 probability `0.044285`. 3–3 becomes 4–3 under full-game OT/SO convention, so for total 6.0 `P(U) = P(reg total ≤5) = 0.507248`, `P(push)=P(reg total=6)−P(reg 3–3)=0.158694−0.044285=0.114409`, `P(Over)=P(reg total≥7)+P(reg 3–3)=0.378343`. Under regulation-only totals, push=0.158694, Over=0.334058 instead. A one-goal OT/SO resolution cannot turn either team +1.5 into a loss if the 60m scores were tied.

**Margins and uncertainty:** independent Skellam 60m Kulager minus Beibarys centre 1.083; SD `2.372` (observed actual variance/empty-net effects UNKNOWN). Full-game final expected total approx `5.784` (regulation total plus probability of tie) with no independently fitted endpoint calibration. Both side handicaps rely on the same Poisson joint, not independent binary picks.

**Sensitivity (predeclared alternate cases for missing goalies/tactical/strength uncertainty):**

| Case | λ Beibarys | λ Kulager | Total reg μ | Kulager +1.5 W | Beibarys +1.5 W | Under 6 W | Over 6 W | Exact-6 push |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| LOW | 2.10 | 3.05 | 5.15 | 86.64% | 60.45% | 58.97% | 30.24% | 10.79% |
| BASE | 2.27 | 3.35 | 5.63 | 86.79% | 57.77% | 50.72% | 37.83% | 11.44% |
| HIGH | 2.55 | 3.70 | 6.25 | 86.08% | 56.25% | 40.64% | 47.88% | 11.48% |
| CLOSE | 2.60 | 2.90 | 5.50 | 78.45% | 70.22% | 52.89% | 36.26% | 10.85% |

These alternative intensities and weights are illustrative, NOT empirical goalie distributions or statistical confidence intervals. The numerical order of the two +1.5 sides stays the same in the listed cases except an extreme close-game scenario may compress the gap. Total direction is unstable (Under vs Over); no high-confidence total claim.

#### A5. Contract-by-contract W/P/L/V, thresholds, line distance

- `kul_plus15`: W=86.79%, P=0, L=13.21%, V=0; p among decisions=86.79%. Exposed if Beibarys wins by 2+; a high hit rate comes from a favourable handicap, not model skill. Price threshold 1.152 decimal.
- `bei_plus15`: W=57.77%, P=0, L=42.23%, V=0; p among decisions=57.77%. Exposed if Kulager wins by 2+ (incl late empty-net). Price threshold 1.731 decimal.
- `under6`: W=50.72%, P=11.44%, L=37.83%, V=0; conditional p=57.28%. Under is modest preferred to Over in base, but near threshold and sensitive to goalie pace; price threshold 1.746 decimal.
- `over6`: W=37.83%, P=11.44%, L=50.72%, V=0; conditional p=42.72%. Over needs ≥7 in full-match convention, including OT/SO 3–3 tie; threshold 2.341 decimal.
- Line distances: signed Kulager-margin +1.5 condition translates to `D_KUL ≥−1`, threshold roughly −1.5 in continuous approximation vs centre +1.083, SD 2.372; A+1.5 means D_KUL ≤1, threshold 1.5. Total 6.0 threshold versus regulator centre 5.627, distance +0.373 goals or +0.157 regulation Poisson SD; OT correction separate. Exact model is integer grid, not a continuity-corrected normal surrogate.
- EV for a **real** offered decimal price `d` cannot be calculated absent `d`, operator terms and acceptance/void rules; at hypothetical decimal 2.00 the W/P/L row arithmetic is only illustrative and is not a recommendation. Outcome stakes are not independent; summing EV of both +1.5 positions requires actual prices and joint bankroll risk.

#### A6. Adjustments, regimes, low/base/high and failure paths

No verified goalie, confirmed attack units, travel/rest roster, special-teams rate, league-average or exact shot quality. Included team/opp current scoring and conceding only; no separate injuries/H2H/home adjustment to avoid double counting. Full-season and L10 blends use one overlapping history; no sampled posterior or calibrated spread. Goalkeeper/scratch uncertainty could shift each λ by multiple tenths and reverse the O/U preference. Specific main false-positive risk: Beibarys two+ victory kills Kulager +1.5; Kulager two+ victory kills home +1.5; empty-net risk is asymmetric on home +1.5. Indoor weather N/A. Travel: Kulager listed away in Atyrau after its last home on 11 Oct (5-day interval); exact travel itinerary UNKNOWN; Beibarys last played away Aktobe 13 Oct (3-day interval), returns home, travel load NOT_VERIFIED. No admitted competition-specific model release/capability (`ML_NOT_RUN`), no frozen same-line `BASELINE_P` or baseline-only shadow; diagnostic ladder of four supplied contracts **analyst-only**, not MODEL_ASSISTED_PRIMARY. The two handicaps can both win when regulation margin is −1/0/+1 (after a tie, full final is ±1), probability 44.56%; they cannot both lose by construction (joint failure mechanically zero). O/U six share one push: complements conditional on action, and **cannot** serve as independent top-two evidence. Historical NHL league-level 2-goal EN rates do not automatically transfer to PHL. Opposite-side ±1.5 handicap ranks are not a proof of diversification or profitable expected value.

| Conditional analyst rank | Exact contract | analyst_p WIN | PUSH | LOSS | Break-even decimal price | Hypothetical EV at d=2.00 |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 1 | Vary ML | **71.99%** | 0.00% | 28.01% | **1.389** | +43.97% |
| 2 | Under 6.0 | **61.17%** | 10.47% | 28.36% | **1.464** | +32.81% |
| 3 | Olomouc +1.5 | **56.41%** | 0.00% | 43.59% | **1.773** | +12.82% |
| 4 | Over 6.0 | **28.36%** | 10.47% | 61.17% | **3.157** | -32.81% |

- **Top-1 conditional analyst direction:** **Karlovy Vary ML** (71.99% W) — better current defence and Olomouc scoring slump. Failure: Olomouc opportunistic scoring, Czech home advantage, Frodl not starting, OT upset after a tied regulation game. The 60-min tie branch is 16.18%, with Karlovy Vary winning 62% of this branch **by assumption**.
- **Top-2 conditional analyst direction:** **Under 6.0** (61.17% W, 10.47% push), with the central expected regulation total 5.025 and Vary's historic goalie dominance. Failure: Olomouc's 9-goal concession on 4 October, penalties/special teams, an unconfirmed goalie, 6-on-5/empty-net and OT with a 3–3 regulation tie. `IH-P2` unconfirmed goalie makes this **ineligible as formal model Rank 1** regardless of estimate.
- **Olomouc +1.5:** 56.41% cover; covers all Olomouc wins and one-goal losses, including a Vary OT/SO win from a tied 60-minute game. Main loss mode is Vary winning by two or more within regulation (model mass 43.59%); pulled goalie on a one-goal deficit can cause that loss. The pair Vary ML + Olomouc +1.5 can both win in a one-goal Vary victory.
- **Over 6.0:** 28.36% win, 10.47% push, 61.17% lose in this model; attack inefficiency and Vary's defence work against it; Olomouc's defensive collapse / backups / power plays support its upper tail.
- **Model selection gate and family controls:** NOT_ASSESSABLE because verified primary p_card, goalie confirmation, operator rule confirmation and fitted Czech competition baseline are unavailable. Printed ranks are descriptive analyst-only; no formal PASS/Rank1/Rank2, no numerical learning updates. Shadow gate / same-line baseline ranks: NONE; `PARTIAL_UNIVERSE` — the four user lines only.
- **Line difficulty:** total threshold 6 is +0.975 goals above unadjusted mixture regulation mean 5.025; conditional final mean 5.187 makes the distance +0.813. Signed Vary minus Olomouc margin mean ≈ +1.20, so Olomouc +1.5 covers margin when visitor margin ≤1 (0.20 below centre); Vary ML corresponds to visitor full margin ≥1, not the same key. z not provided because a calibrated residual SD is unavailable; Poisson implied width is NOT equivalent to empirical calibration.

### A4. Arithmetic, construction and model/line baselines

**Actual execution/capability:** `python -m ml capability` attempted within session, failed `No module named ml` in `/opt/pyvenv/bin/python`; a public GitHub read-only code view cannot execute the pinned model. `ml` environment/competition release/history input/actual execution receipt absent. **No primary training, inference, Hedge weights, learning update or verified execution.** A true model-issued probability would require actual archived execution bundle, not this arithmetic.

**Sport-scoped reproducible analyst fallback (not a model substitution).** Original season rates: Olomouc scored 18/10=1.80 and conceded 36/10=3.60; Vary scored 22/9=2.444 and conceded 16/9=1.778. Seasonal attack/opposition-defence centre = (1.80+1.778)/2 = **1.789** Olomouc; (2.444+3.60)/2 = **3.022** Vary. Venue-specific Olomouc-at-home versus Vary-on-road centre = (2.00+2.20)/2 = **2.10** Olomouc; (2.00+4.80)/2 = **3.40** Vary. A declared 70% seasonal / 30% venue blend yields approx **1.88 / 3.14**; rounded working BASE scenario 1.90 / 3.10. This is a subjective smoothing operation, NOT point-in-time league baseline fit. Missing on-ice shots, xG, confirmed starter and hockey efficiency components means the weights are not statistically fitted.

**Sensitivity distribution:** regulation goals independent Poisson(lambda__OL), Poisson(lambda__KVA), with score grid 0..26 on both teams (truncation mass negligible at these means). Scenarios low/base/high model goalie/regime/scoring uncertainty without falsely assigning a named starter. Default mixture weights 0.25/0.50/0.25. A regulation tie (model 16.18% mixture) produces one additional official winning goal to Olomouc with probability 0.38 and to Vary with 0.62, **explicit hypothesis**. `W`/`P`/`L` of all rows derive from one distribution, and scenario probabilities sum to 1 within numeric rounding. Final match goal total is regulation sum +1 in the regulation-tie branch; for 3–3 at 60 minutes the final total is 7 (Over 6.0), while 2–2 becomes 5 (Under 6.0). Exactly six final goals from non-tied regulation outcomes = PUSH.

| Scenario (weight) | Olomouc reg λ | Vary reg λ | Vary ML | Olomouc +1.5 | Under 6.0 W | Exactly 6 push | Over 6.0 W |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| LOW | 1.55 | 2.65 | 72.12% | 58.97% | 75.31% | 8.55% | 16.14% |
| BASE | 1.90 | 3.10 | 72.00% | 56.31% | 61.60% | 10.80% | 27.61% |
| HIGH | 2.30 | 3.60 | 71.84% | 54.04% | 46.19% | 11.73% | 42.08% |
| MIX | 1.91 | 3.11 | 71.99% | 56.41% | 61.17% | 10.47% | 28.36% |

**Exact settlement/action and EV maths:** Without actual operator quote, **actual market EV = NOT_ESTIMATED**. The fair decimal threshold for rows with pushes is `1 + P(loss)/P(win)`; with no push it equals `1/P(win)`. At hypothetical **decimal odds 2.00** the expected net profit per $1 staked equals `P(win) − P(loss)`; a push returns stake with $0 profit. The expected return at ANY provided decimal price d is `EV_per_$1 = W×(d−1) − L` (push has zero profit). A positive modeled value is not certified real value: estimate is uncalibrated and operator price unknown.

**No posterior certification or measured confidence interval:** scenario ranges and weights express analyst assumptions; not a validated prediction interval. Individual goalie GAAs and SV% are historic context; no unsupported conversion of 97.88% save percentage into a guaranteed scoring suppression.

### A5. Failure routes, candidate assessment and dependency

- **Vary ML 71.99%:** team results and defensive advantage support the favoured visitor. Olomouc's home venue, goalie variance, special teams and opponent-score late states oppose; a 60-minute tie occurs 16.18% in this model and remains undecided until the OT/SO branch, where Olomouc upsets the visitor in 38% of ties under the stated assumption.
- **Olomouc +1.5 56.41%:** wins if Olomouc wins or loses by one; complements probability of **Vary win by 2+** (43.59%) only under chosen full-match endpoint and no-void. Some Vary ML wins are simultaneously Olomouc +1.5 wins, so the two are not opposites. P(Vary ML loses AND Olomouc +1.5 loses) = 0 (mechanically protected under same endpoint); ML/total cross-quantity joint is NOT_COMPUTED.
- **Under6 61.17% / Push 10.47% / Loss 28.36%:** low-mean + disciplined goaltending route, but a high-scoring Olomouc defensive breakdown and pulled-goalie route destroy Under. Operator OT treatment can switch winners at tie-score boundaries.
- **Over6 28.36% / Push 10.47% / Loss 61.17%:** same count object, complement on DECISIVE mass only. H2H and five recent games are background, not a second independent adjustment.
- **Potential EV** before odds is a *fair-price criterion*, not a market value verdict. All four rows retained even though primary four-row eligibility fails.

1. Regular-season Czech ELH: 3-on-3 OT for five minutes if regulation tied; shootout if needed. Source: [APK LH system rules](https://www.apklh.cz/system-souteze) and [2026/27 league season rules](https://www.livesport.cz/zpravy/hokej-tipsport-extraliga-2026-2027-rozpis-program-info/SE00jy54/). Operator-specific settlement must be inspected independently.

3. Goalie unresolved: Frodl vs Král and Machovský vs Cichoň are historical appearance profiles, not starter certification; start or emergency substitution affects margin and total. Special-teams/PP/PK and full match benches not extracted.
4. Empty-net branches not separately parameterised: regulation Poisson+OT score grid understates structural late pulled-goalie dependence in plus puck-line loss. No fake precision claim.
5. For a conditional full-game 6.0 goal Asian total, score=6 pushes, and 3–3 after 60 minutes goes to 7 if the official result appends one winner goal. If operator instead uses 60 minutes only, all probabilities require a NEW distribution and cannot be taken as issued.

### A4. Numerical methods, baseline and calculation (NOT_PRIMARY)

- Full-time LOW/BASE/HIGH independent Poisson Boca goal means = 1.15/1.49/1.70, Talleres = .80/.79/1.02; FT λ=1.95/2.28/2.72. The base 1.49/.79 comes from domestic 2026 multi-window GF/GA, home/away and player availability analyst adjustment, not a fit. LOW assumes more rotation/sterile possession, HIGH assumes stronger Boca chance conversion and/or more open Talleres game. No data-derived scenario weights; default 0.25/0.5/0.25 selected explicitly.
- Equation `P(Under 2.5 | lambda) = exp(-lambda) * (1 + lambda + lambda^2 / 2)`, with lambda=λ_Boca+λ_Talleres; Over complementary. Score grid exact up to 17 each (mass remainder negligible) for three-way win/draw/loss.
- Separate half process: Boca home attack H1 .57 and Talleres away H1 concede .14 imply rough matchup Boca H1 ≈(.57+.14)/2=.355; Talleres away H1 attack .36 and Boca home H1 concede .29 imply Talleres H1 ≈.325. Naive venue aggregate `.680`; analyst base `.77` includes modest league/fixture uncertainty shrink without independently fitted phase effects; LOW `.59`, HIGH `1.02`. `P(H1 Under 0.5)=exp(-λ_H1)`; period process not linked by simulation to full score, so exact joint family failure is unavailable.
- Corners: base venue ingredients predict `(6.14+5.14)/2+(3.29+2.71)/2=8.64`. Analyst centre **BASE 8.75** (rounded+league context), with LOW `7.9`, HIGH `9.8`; corners count `NegBin(r=24, mean=μ)`, variance `μ+μ²/24`. For each k≥0, `P(C=k)=Γ(k+24)/(Γ(24)k!) * (24/(24+μ))^24 * (μ/(24+μ))^k`; Under 10.5 = Σ k=0..10. Shape=24 is an unsupported analogue for width, not fitted for Argentina; bounded source/provider sensitivity in A5.
- **Arithmetic receipts:** `calculate_and_retain.py` creates `ALL_SIX_CONTRACT_PROBABILITIES.csv`, `SENSITIVITY_SCENARIOS.csv`, `FORM_WINDOWS.json`, `CALCULATION_RECEIPT.json`; script saved alongside exports. Numerical mixture means: full goals 2.3075, first-half 0.7875, corners 8.80; all three pairs sum exactly to 1, individual 90-minute score grid home/draw/away sums to 1 within displayed precision.
- **Current primary ML/RL:** GitHub supports a contextual `ml.workflow` model for supported competition-specific training releases of ≥30 admitted same-league events. Here the checkout/module and reviewed release are unavailable: `python -m ml capability` exit code1 `No module named ml`; `/mnt/data` no checkout. ### A4. Reproducible run distribution, independent heuristic (NOT_PRIMARY)

1. Run centre anchor: season TB 4.330 / CLE 4.099; adjusted toward TB Martinez superior 3.10 ERA, Cleveland opener/bulk-innings uncertainty, home venue and October batting. **Adjustments are explicitly subjective, not estimated ML coefficients:** provisional base TB λ=4.20, CLE λ=3.65. Low 3.70 / 3.20; high 4.80 / 4.10. Priors 25% / 50% / 25%. Overall E[TB]=**4.225**, E[CLE]=**3.650**, E[total]=**7.875** runs; mean margin TB +0.575.
2. Within each scenario, 9-inning score marginals independent `NB(size=2.8, mean=λ)`; `P(X=x)=NB.pmf(x,2.8,2.8/(2.8+λ))` for x=0,...,45. Dispersion `k=2.8` is an **analyst assumption**, not empirically fitted. Mixture provides additional width. Actual MLB MLB score correlations and dynamic inning states unknown.
3. To illustrate extras, if X=Y after nine, add ONE run to the eventual winner with `P(TB wins extras|tie)=λTB/(λTB+λCLE)`, then recompute every displayed margin and total from the same corrected joint states. This ignores potential extra innings with >1 run, homer walk-offs, 9th-inning home-batting truncation, sustained bullpen/tie stress and unusual endings. No automatic runner in playoff extras. **9-inning tie probability 10.85%**. This approximation can materially affect integer totals; do not use it as a certified forecast.
4. Code recipe and exact scenario calculator in `/tmp/build_tb_cle.py` local execution and JSON/CSV outputs included. Truncation to 45 runs per side leaves <5e-8 omitted mass. Test complement symmetry `TB ML + CLE ML ≈1`, `Under 7.5 + Over 7.5 ≈1`, `TB +1.5 >= TB ML`; see calculation readback.
5. No input is conditioned on game result. However **first play not verified**; cannot call issue a certified clean pregame card. Actual market odds absent and excluded from SPORTS_ONLY research.

CLE Armstrong has zero regular-season starts; he may pitch 1 inning and hand off to Cantillo/Bibee/Cecconi. Using Armstrong's 4.58 ERA as a 6-inning starter forecast would be a serious error.
- **Lineups:** Both official batting orders posted; ALCS hitters fluctuate in <10 game exposure. TB offense 3.0 runs/game across 6 postseason games, CLE 4.25 over 8. Postseason compression and pitcher matchup matter, but no false 60-minute freeze or invented injuries.
- **Bullpen fatigue/late separation:** CLE bullpen used high leverage in G3; TB bullpen preserved some late arms. Exact pitcher availability not verified. MLB narrow leads, walk-offs and inning states invalidate an ordinary symmetric tied nine-inning Poisson forecast; model scenarios are wide, not exact.
- **Weather/termination:** MLB Communications says 8:08 PM EDT expected first pitch delayed for rain; no certified actual first pitch. Rain can cause postponement, suspension, or altered bullpen strategy. No fabricated full-game termination probabilities, runner rules, or game-specific wind adjustment.
- **Scenario probability sensitivity:** LOW/BASE/HIGH printed in `SCENARIO_GRID.csv` for all named contracts. Non-qualified candidate ordering can flip if weather forces reliever-heavy innings or lineup changes.
- **Risk:** selection q, ROI and CYCLE weights `NOT_AVAILABLE`; league-only baseline identity not released, all analyst rows have `p_card=NOT_ESTIMATED` and belong to NOT_PRIMARY.

| HIGH | 0.25 | 4.60 | 5.35 | 57.04% | 57.47% | 63.12% |

Mixture weights .25/.50/.25, team mean centres weighted LG 4.1125, KIA 4.8000; raw nine-inning combined 8.9125. No trained competition-release `BASELINE_P` available and no qualified KBO postseason calibration/dispersion; numeric reference only from KBO 2026 regular season. `TEAM_BASELINE_P=UNVALIDATED_KBO_POSTSEASON` even if raw season PPG can be computed.

Scenario Over 8.5 spans 41.92–63.12% and LG+1.5 spans 57.04–59.91%. This is **assumption sensitivity**, not confidence intervals or calibrated uncertainty. Missing KBO park total census, bullpen stochastic earned/unearned runs, home walkoff truncation and conditional weather; extra-inning kernel approximated. No same-game actual scoreboard stats used.

### A4. Statistical coherence, settlement and failures

- `P(Over 8.5)+P(Under 8.5)=1`; `P(LG+1.5)+P(KIA−1.5)=1` given a completed scored game incl tie (+1.5 covers a final draw). 9-inning draw is neither a full-match moneyline loss nor an automatic refund; 15-inning tie is a separate decision state. Operator action may change runline tie interpretation. Simulation grid 56×56 states with renormalisation; numerical tail beyond ~55 negligible given mean and NB8.
- Core outcome paths: KIA leads 2+ after 9 and preserves it; LG +1.5 fails from that or bullpen collapse. KIA loses on LG's offense against Naile, fielding and bullpen; KIA ML can win by 1 while LG +1.5 also wins. Under8.5 loses on combined 9+ including late extras; Over fails when Naile and Tolhurst both work deep and scoring suppressed.
- Postseason tied game can go through 15, then draw; neutral stake refund depends sportsbook. Game suspended/no-game policies are unspecified. Not a standard 12-inning regular-season tie; don't combine with MLB ghost runners.
- No actual bookmaker decimal odds were supplied; **expected monetary value NOT_ESTIMATED**, model fair-odds / hypothetical 2.00 illustrative only. If odds `d` provided then `EV per stake = p_win*(d−1) − p_loss`, with p_loss excluding actual refund/push/void mass.
- Frozen original issued probabilities are immutable on later settlement; no automatic grade from score exposed after kickoff. Recheck final from official gamecenter only on explicit settlement.

### A5. Pre- and post-scheduled timing and known-at protections

The date-stamped team games and lineup announcements predate scheduled 14:00 KST, but individual secondary sites are dynamically updated and their web publication timestamp for the exact raw score row is not source-frozen. Card was issued at `2026-10-17T16:06:17+11:00`, **after scheduled 16:00 AEDT**; actual first play and whether score exposed at issue not verified. Therefore forecast has `TIMING_UNVERIFIED` and no certified pregame cohort. The card does **not** use a 17-Oct live score or outcome as a model feature. If official first pitch was before issue, this is a post-start analytic reconstruction, not pregame validation.

### A2 — Decision summary: supplied universe and ranked eligible rows

Because it would otherwise be first, it is **INELIGIBLE_FOR_PRIMARY_RANK1** and retained in the diagnostic universe, not misleadingly moved to Rank 2. - First-half, site/role-specific **current 2026 league**: Beijing home n=13: GF 10, GA 4 first-half; Qingdao away n=13: GF 6, GA 10 first-half. First-half combined over 0.5 occurred in approximately 69% of Beijing home fixtures and 69% of Qingdao away fixtures, according to published season rates. The two records describe overlapping *team populations*, not 26 independent matchup simulations. Source: https://www.soccerstats.com/pmatch.asp?league=china&stats=223-16-14-2026 .
- Corners: 24score separate *actual recorded corner counts* (not prices): Beijing full-season 27 matches, mean total 10.63; Beijing home n=13, mean combined 10.62. Qingdao full-season n=27 mean total 9.30; Qingdao away n=13 mean total 10.00. Recent ten-game means 10.3 Beijing and 7.9 Qingdao. These rates are from provider's corner tables; venue means are not a full exact-venue covariance estimate. Source: https://m.24score.com/football/match/844270-beijing_guoan-qingdao_west_coast .
- Sample prior actual corner count rows: 9-Oct Hainiu–Beijing 2+4=6; 5-Sep Shanghai Port–Beijing 14+1=15; 28-Aug Dalian–Beijing 4+1=5; 22-Aug Beijing–Yunnan 9+5=14; 18-Aug Shenhua–Beijing 8+5=13; 11-Oct Qingdao–Dalian 3+3=6; 5-Sep Wuhan–Qingdao 6+4=10; 29-Aug Tianjin–Qingdao 5+6=11; 22-Aug Qingdao–Shandong 3+4=7; 14-Aug Qingdao–Chongqing 3+4=7. Same 24score source, additional data lineage not proven independent.
- Most recent H2H 19-May 2026 was Qingdao West Coast 1–1 Beijing, HT 0–0, full corners 3–9 (12); H2H as weak contextual illustration, not enough to set baseline. Sources https://fbref.com/en/stathead/matchup/teams/d87e75be/8ab37ab8/Qingdao-West-Coast-vs-Beijing-Guoan-History and https://m.24score.com/football/match/844270-beijing_guoan-qingdao_west_coast .
- xG/shot quality adjusted for opponents: `NOT_DERIVED`, no verified exact-match and same-competition xG sample located; GF/GA are explicitly raw realised-goal proxies and not masqueraded as expected goals.

### A4 — Coherent hand probability construction, four supplied market rows plus phase pair

#### Regulation-goal family (independent half and team Poisson approximations)

1. Unadjusted team goal means: Beijing from season home scoring and Qingdao away conceding = (27/13 + 23/13)/2 = **1.9231**, about 1.925; Qingdao from season away scoring and Beijing home conceding = (14/13 + 18/13)/2 = **1.2308**. Total unadjusted **3.154**.
2. Availability/rest **subjective** Beijing adjustment: Buriram travel/possible Yang Liyu absence −0.08 goals, return/rested Zhang Yuning +0.05 goals, net −0.03 => Beijing **1.90**; Qingdao **1.23**, total **3.13**. No assertion of measured player impact or opponent-adjusted xG. No numerical Qingdao medical adjustment (unverified).
3. First-half attack from exact site-and-innings splits: Beijing `(10/13 + 10/13)/2 = 0.7692`; Qingdao `(6/13 + 4/13)/2 = 0.3846`; aggregate **1.1538**, rounded to **1.155** for calculation. Second-half centres defined as residual Beijing `1.90 − .770=1.130`, Qingdao `1.23 − .385=.845`. Thus half-by-team processes sum to the full team goal means. Independent Poisson increments and teams are simplifying assumptions, with possible shared tempo/low-score correction MISSING.
4. Full-game goals `G = GB + GQ ~ Poisson(3.13)`; P(total 0–2) = `exp(−3.13) [1 + 3.13 + (3.13)^2/2]` = `~0.04372 × 9.02845 = 0.395`; P(Over 2.5) = **0.605**. This is a 90-min result, no overtime mixture.
5. First-half goals `H ~ Poisson(1.155)`; P(Under 0.5)=`exp(−1.155)=0.315`; Over 0.5=**0.685**. The 1H variable is explicitly coupled to regulation full-time as `G=H + G_second`, not an unlinked substitute.
6. Winner: independent team goal Poisson(1.90) vs Poisson(1.23), summing shared scoreline states: Beijing victory **~0.530**, exact draw **~0.224**, Qingdao victory **~0.246** (sums 1.000 at shown precision). Home signed margin expected +0.67, exact integer scoreline implied. 90-minute draw not voided in three-way winner; two-way action convention unknown and no two-way contract is ranked.

#### Provider-backed separate corner-count family

1. Relevant location 13-game combined-corner averages: Beijing home **10.62**, Qingdao away **10.00**; mean **10.31**. Last-ten-match combined means Beijing **10.3**, Qingdao **7.9**, mean **9.10**.
2. To avoid a stale-season-only anchor, analyst recipe is predeclared: **one-third location sample + two-thirds last-ten sample**, `λ_C = (1/3)(10.31)+(2/3)(9.10)=9.503`, round **9.50**. This arbitrary weighting is a source-backed *assumption*, not a fitted corner model. Other weights are tested in sensitivity.
3. Corners `C ~ Poisson(9.50)` independent of goal counts **only for marginal pricing**; no cross-quantity joint is claimed. The distribution is analytically normalized on all nonnegative integers. P(Under 10.5)=`Σ_(k=0)^10 exp(−9.5)×9.5^k/k!` ≈ **0.645**. Checkpoints `P(C=9)≈0.1300, P(C=10)≈0.1235`; cumulative through 10 ≈0.645. Therefore P(Over 10.5)=**0.355**.
4. This Poisson may understate corner-count overdispersion. Variance, team/team and goal/corner dependence are not measured and the exact count provider settlement definition is unconfirmed. The corner choice is therefore unusually fragile.

**Precision:** Poisson recurrence and 3-decimal display give an effective probability precision around ±0.002 (with greater uncertainty from *assumptions*); comparisons use unrounded intermediate values conceptually. All six W/P/L/V: no ties/half-point pushes on normally completed fixtures; W=p, L=1-p, P=0, V=0 *conditional on action*, with action probability itself not estimated. Complements sum to one; H and G share an explicit nested mechanism.

#### League-only comparator attempt

Published prematchstats league context reports a 2026 CSL mean of **3.11 goals per game** from **217 matches** and Over 2.5 at **58%**: https://prematchstats.com/china/chinese-super-league/beijing-guoan-vs-qingdao-west-coast-stats/15552654 . If 3.11 were a justified Poisson league-only intensity, its *illustrative* same-line Over 2.5 would be `1−exp(−3.11)[1+3.11+(3.11²/2)]≈0.60`. **Do not treat this as an official frozen `BASELINE_LEAGUE_V1`:** individual 217 match IDs, independent corroboration and strict known-at cutoff were not reconciled. `BASELINE_P = NOT_FROZEN_DATE_VERIFIED` for all rows, and exact CSL league-only corners and halftime inputs remain `NOT_ESTIMATED`. No placeholder .50 baseline.

### A5 — Frozen candidates, ranking policy, sensitivities, dependence and gates

- Candidate universe frozen to **six** named directions supplied by user (1H O/U0.5, FT goals O/U2.5, FT corners O/U10.5); no invented alternate lines or player props. All six explicitly priced once from their own physical quantities. No selection above ceiling 0.90.
- The rank-1 `1H Over 0.5` special family rule mandates half-specific evidence **AND** 0.72 minimum **AND** 0.05 lead if it would be rank1. Its empirical 1H rates support about .685 but not .720, so `FAMILY_RANK1_INELIGIBLE`, diagnostic only. Removing it, selected ranking: Corners U10.5 0.645, FT O2.5 0.605, FT U2.5 0.395, Corners O10.5 0.355. Rank 3/4 are opposites, NOT extra independent recommendations.
- Rank-1 numeric gate: probability ~0.645 passes 0.62 floor; strongest eligible non-complementary alternative FT O2.5 ~0.605, gap **~0.040**, indistinguishable from the 0.04 threshold within 2ε≈0.004. **`GATE_UNRESOLVED_PRECISION`**, not PASS. Separate `SHADOW_GATE_RET03` (other lines on same *corners* quantity removed): same FT O2.5 competitor, likewise unresolved. Rank-2 0.605 ≥0.58, nominal pass.
- Rank-2 shadow different quantity `SHADOW_R2_RET04`: FT goals Over 2.5, same as selected rank2. `SHADOW_R2_XCARD6`: FT goals Over 2.5; no exact goal/corner joint derived. `SHADOW_RANK_DELTA`: NONE, no verified league-only baseline for two eligible rows. `SHADOW_BASE_ONLY`: same goal baseline before small player rest change, but not separately certified as mixture from material branches.
- Failure of both top two = `(corners ≥11) ∩ (goals ≤2)`. Marginal losses 0.355 and 0.395; **Fréchet bounds [0, 0.355]**. No unjustified product 0.355×0.395. The historical 0.30 target / 0.35 warning cannot be decisively evaluated because joint correlation is unmeasured. Both complementary pairs are `FORCED_PAIR` and excluded as independent Hit@2 evidence.
- Total-goal Over2.5 line distance from mean: `3.13−2.5=+0.63 goals`. Corners Under10.5 distance: `10.5−9.5=+1.0 corner`; 1H Over0.5 distance `1.155−0.5=+0.655 goals`. Discrete threshold cdfs computed via integer events, not normal z.

| Sensitivity case | FT goal λ | P(O2.5) | HT λ | P(HT O0.5) | Corner λ | P(U10.5) | Mechanism / caution |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Low-scoring, corner-suppressing | 2.70 | ~0.506 | 0.95 | ~0.613 | 8.5 | ~0.760 | Defences/absences inhibit finishing and corner demand |
| **Base** | **3.13** | **0.605** | **1.155** | **0.685** | **9.5** | **0.645** | Current season, site split and chosen weighting |
| High-scoring, high-corner | 3.60 | ~0.697 | 1.35 | ~0.741 | 10.5 | ~0.521 | Fast match, efficient finishing and repeated wide attacks |

Separate *unadjusted* scoring mean 3.154 implies Over2.5 about 0.611, vs base about 0.605. Corner venue-only mean 10.31 gives Under10.5 about 0.55, while recent-only mean 9.10 gives about 0.69. Therefore use `ADJUSTMENT_DEPENDENT`: a defensible alternate corner weighting can change which row ranks first or whether Rank1 reaches 0.62. Sensitivity ranges are scenarios, not confidence intervals.

### A6 — Tactical/evidence synthesis, failure paths, winner

1. Beijing's current-season home scoring 2.08 per match meets Qingdao away concessions 1.77; however Qingdao's season-long draw rate (14/27) and the 1–1 reverse fixture warn against treating home victory as certain.
2. Beijing's 18-event all-competition unbeaten narrative includes ACL and Cup opponents; exact-competition L20 is separately displayed so those are not misclassified as 20 CSL results. Beijing recent 3–0 and Qingdao recent 0–4 are context, not sole projection anchors.
3. Beijing's overseas travel may suppress late attacking intensity; Zhang Yuning's return potentially offsets some of that, and Yang Liyu's old injury flare could reduce wing depth. Numeric magnitudes are not sourced player impact figures.
4. Qingdao's defensive ability varies: 0–0 against Henan and Chongqing in August, 0–4 against Dalian in October. A sustained low block could suppress 1H and total goals, while conceding corners to Beijing pressure.
5. Beijing strong home attacking means goals may exceed 2.5, but 0–0/1–1 routes remain tangible. The margin winner estimate is **Beijing 53.0%, draw 22.4%, Qingdao 24.6%** (Poisson approximation).
6. Corners may exceed 10.5 even with fewer than three goals if one side trails and generates repeat attacks, or remain below if play is central or conversion rapid. A goal model alone cannot size corners; the separate actual-corners provider is essential.
7. Starting XIs, late replacements, goalkeeper fitness and the pitch have not been verified. This is **LINEUP_UNCONFIRMED**, not fully evidenced pregame prediction. A late reported XI cannot be assumed known before research cutoff unless its own publication time is demonstrated.

## Deep loss review · P-584 · TOP_TOTAL / RANK_1_DIAGNOSTIC · Under 4.5

**1. Exact frozen claim.** Regulation Under 4.5, p 0.8794, arbitrary three-case independent-Poisson scenario, NOT_PRIMARY. Baseline unavailable. Issue and input timing unverified; no ledger or executed reference is backfilled.
**2. Verified outcome.** Six regulation goals, checked from the structured first/second-half totals against club corroboration; six exceeds 4.5 by 1.5. Capture timestamps are in SOURCE_DISCOVERY_REGISTER.csv; final whistle is unknown.
**3. Distribution check.** Mean 2.55, SD 1.637; z +2.107514. The frozen mixture with scales 0.8/1/1.2 and weights 0.25/0.50/0.25 assigns P(total >=6)=0.049757. The lost original row scores Brier 0.773344360 and log loss 2.115275995. Reference/baseline comparison is NOT_COMPUTABLE.
**4. Error decomposition.** The scenario's total centre was 3.45 below the final. The shared scale scenarios were arbitrary uncertainty, rather than confirmed-player effects or an empirically calibrated tail. No blame percentage or newly fitted variance is claimed.
**5. Research audit.** Exact event and final now VERIFIED. Original team means, current players, publication chronology and complete opponent-adjusted input set remain UNVERIFIED_AT_CUTOFF without their source captures. The card itself disclosed arbitrary scales and unconfirmed roles. New club/score pages do not retroactively close those gaps.
**6. What decided the game.** Dragones scored twice before halftime and three times after it; Guerreros scored once after halftime. This is the admitted evidence. Conflicting minute timelines and unverified red-card timing are not used to manufacture a causal account. A pre-cutoff season-rate comparison remains unavailable.
**7. Knowable at cutoff?** Frozen model assumptions and line were knowable. Six goals and their realised allocation were not. Missed team news is NOT_ASSESSABLE without a pre-cutoff publication capture.
**8. Process audit.** Endpoint and grade are correct under the frozen regulation convention. The numerical precision and 87.94% confidence lacked a validated reference or calibration study; original output stayed diagnostic. Under 3.5 shares the same total exposure.
**9. Verdict.** MIXED: a quantified upper-tail outcome plus unvalidated centre/tail construction and incomplete evidence. The original held primary status correctly; this should not have been issued as a certified 87.94% primary forecast. One loss cannot quantify persistent miscalibration.
**10. Cutoff-only counterfactual.** NOT_ESTIMATED: no newly inferred lineup, match outcome or missing reference is priced retrospectively.
**11. Failure class and recurrence.** Unverified input admission / uncalibrated total centre and width. Comparable recurrence count NOT_COMPUTED; no historical pattern is invented.
**12. Correction.** Prompt 8's exact-scope reference and adjustment checks now apply to future cards, and prospective qualification needs 50 paired games and eight weeks. Any predictive change needs its own frozen lane; old p values remain unchanged.
**13. Matched wins.** Home-or-draw and Over 1.5 won with the same unverified input and scenario limitations; their wins do not validate the Under or erase its loss.
**14. Honesty.** Original numbers and labels preserved; no causal shares, retroactive publication, operator terms or baseline invented. Source conflicts retained. Preservation/disclosure PASS; full information closure INCOMPLETE.
